\displaystyle(S_{a_{1}a_{2}})^{\cdots\,\underline{r}\,\cdots}_{\cdots\,\underline{s}\,\cdots}\;P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}^{\underline{s}\,\underline{t}}

\displaystyle=P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}^{\underline{r}\,\underline{s}}\;(S_{a_{1}a_{2}})^{\cdots\,\underline{s}\,\cdots}_{\cdots\,\underline{t}\,\cdots}\,.

(384)

Note that these relations do
not
involve conjugation of representation labels. As a corollary, one obtains

\displaystyle P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}^{\underline{r}_{1}\underline{s}}\;S_{a_{1}a_{2}\phantom{R}}^{\underline{s}\,\underline{r}_{2},\underline{t}_{1}\underline{t}_{2}}

\displaystyle=S_{a_{1}a_{2}\phantom{R}}^{\underline{r}_{1}\underline{r}_{2},\underline{s}\,\underline{t}_{2}}\;P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}^{\underline{s}\,\underline{t}_{1}}

(385)

and its analogue for the second parton.
Using these results and the rules in (
366
), one can generalise equations (
102
) to (
4.4
) to all valid combinations of representations. We find that for the collinear soft factor the first two representations must be conjugate to the second two,

\displaystyle{}^{R_{1}R_{2},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}S_{a_{1}a_{2}}

\displaystyle=\delta_{R_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}\,\delta_{R_{2}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}\,{}^{R_{1}R_{2},\overline{R}_{1}\overline{R}_{2}}S_{a_{1}a_{2}}\,,

(386)

and that for singlet and octet channels, the soft factors are equal for the quark and gluon representations. We thus get unity in the singlet sector,

\displaystyle{}^{1}\mskip-1.5muS

\displaystyle={}^{11,11}\mskip-1.5muS_{qq}={}^{11,11}\mskip-1.5muS_{qg}={}^{11,11}\mskip-1.5muS_{gq}={}^{11,11}\mskip-1.5muS_{gg}=1\,,

(387)

and three non-trivial factors

\displaystyle{}^{8}\mskip-1.5muS

\displaystyle={}^{88,88}\mskip-1.5muS_{qq}={}^{8A,8A}\mskip-1.5muS_{qg}={}^{8S,8S}\mskip-1.5muS_{qg}={}^{A8,A8}\mskip-1.5muS_{gq}={}^{S8,S8}\mskip-1.5muS_{gq}

\displaystyle={}^{AA,AA}\mskip-1.5muS_{gg}={}^{SS,SS}\mskip-1.5muS_{gg}={}^{AS,AS}\mskip-1.5muS_{gg}={}^{S\mskip-1.5muA,S\mskip-1.5muA}\mskip-1.5muS_{gg}\,,

\displaystyle{}^{10}\mskip-1.5muS

\displaystyle={}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$10\phantom{\overline{1}}\hskip-3.01389pt$}\mskip 1.5mu\scalebox{0.7}{$\overline{10}$},\scalebox{0.7}{$\overline{10}$}\mskip 1.5mu\scalebox{0.7}{$10\phantom{\overline{1}}\hskip-3.01389pt$}}\mskip-1.5muS_{gg}={}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$\overline{10}$}\,\scalebox{0.7}{$10\phantom{\overline{1}}\hskip-3.01389pt$},\scalebox{0.7}{$10\phantom{\overline{1}}\hskip-3.01389pt$}\,\scalebox{0.7}{$\overline{10}$}}\mskip-1.5muS_{gg}\,,

\displaystyle{}^{27}\mskip-1.5muS

\displaystyle={}^{27\,27,27\,27}\mskip-1.5muS_{gg}\,,

(388)

where for the decuplet sector we used (
382
). Analogous results hold for the Collins-Soper Kernel
J
for collinear DPDs. On the left-hand side of (
I.1
), we have labelled the soft factors by one representative of the different labels on the right-hand sides. Taking a different representative, one has
{}^{8}\mskip-1.5muS={}^{A}\mskip-1.5muS={}^{S}\mskip-1.5muS
and
{}^{10}\mskip-1.5muS={}^{\overline{10}}\mskip-1.5muS
.

The extended soft factor can be expressed in terms of the one for colour singlet production, and in generalisation of (
4.4
), we have

\displaystyle P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}\overline{R}_{1}\overline{R}_{2}}^{\underline{r}_{1}\underline{r}_{2}}\,P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}^{\underline{u}_{1}\underline{u}_{2}}\,P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}\overline{R}_{3}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{3}}^{\underline{s}_{1}\underline{t}_{1}}\,P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}\overline{R}_{4}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{4}}^{\underline{s}_{2}\underline{t}_{2}}\,(S_{a_{1}a_{2}})^{\underline{r}_{1}\underline{r}_{2},\underline{u}_{1}\underline{u}_{2}}_{\underline{s}_{1}\underline{s}_{2},\underline{t}_{1}\underline{t}_{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\delta_{R_{1}\overline{R}_{3}}\,\delta_{R_{2}\overline{R}_{4}}\,\delta_{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{3}}\,\delta_{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{4}}\;m(R_{1})\,{}^{R_{1}}\mskip-1.5muS\,,

(389)

where the combination of tensors on the left and Kronecker deltas on the right implies that all representations must have the same multiplicity. This can be used to correct the argument in section
4.5
for the production of final states with net colour. In the definition (
4.5
), the order of colour indices in
H_{ab}
must be interchanged (see figure
3
), so that

\displaystyle X=H_{a_{1}b_{1}}^{\underline{t}_{1}\underline{s}_{1}}\,H_{a_{2}b_{2}}^{\underline{t}_{2}\underline{s}_{2}}\,

\displaystyle\bigl[F^{T}_{\text{us},b_{1}b_{2}}(Y_{R})\,S^{-1}(Y_{R}-Y_{L})\bigr]^{\underline{r}_{1}\underline{r}_{2}}\,

\displaystyle\times\bigl[S(Y_{R}-Y_{L})\bigr]^{\underline{r}_{1}\underline{r}_{2},\underline{u}_{1}\underline{u}_{2}}_{\underline{s}_{1}\underline{s}_{2},\underline{t}_{1}\underline{t}_{2}}\,\bigr[S^{-1}(Y_{R}-Y_{L})\,F_{\text{us},a_{1}a_{2}}(Y_{L})\bigr]^{\underline{u}_{1}\underline{u}_{2}}\,.

(390)

Correcting (
4.5
) to include all relevant colour channels, we obtain as a replacement of (
4.5
) the final expression

\displaystyle X

\displaystyle=\sum_{R_{1}R_{2}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}{}^{\overline{R}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}H_{a_{1}b_{1}}\,{}^{\overline{R}_{2}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}H_{a_{2}b_{2}}\,{}^{\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}F_{b_{1}b_{2}}\,{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}

(391)

with hard-scattering factors

\displaystyle{}^{RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\!H_{ab}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mathcal{N}_{a}\,\mathcal{N}_{b}}\,\frac{1}{\varepsilon(R)\,\varepsilon(R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime})}\,\frac{1}{m(R)}\,P_{\overline{R}\mskip 1.5mu\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}^{\underline{s}\,\underline{t}}\,H_{ab}^{\underline{s}\,\underline{t}}\,.

(392)

For colour singlet final states, one has
{}^{RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\!H_{ab}=\delta_{R\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\,\eta(R)\,H_{ab}\big/(\mathcal{N}_{a}\,\mathcal{N}_{b})
with
H_{ab}
defined in (
116
). Then (
391
) reduces to (
378
).

Evolution of DPDs.

The colour structure of DPDF and DTMD evolution equations and their solutions must be adjusted for the number of representation labels and the difference between a representation and its conjugate.

The Collins-Soper kernel for DTMDs must have four representation labels, as does the soft factor from which it is derived. The Collins-Soper equation (
128
) then becomes

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\log\zeta}{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}(x_{i},\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta)

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}{}^{R_{1}R_{2},\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}K_{a_{1}a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i})\,{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}(x_{i},\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta)

(393)

with a kernel satisfying

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\log\mu_{1}}\,{}^{R_{1}R_{2},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}K_{a_{1}a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i})

\displaystyle={}-\gamma_{K,a_{1}}(\mu_{1})\,\delta_{R_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}\,\delta_{R_{2}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}

(394)

and a corresponding equation for
\mu_{2}
. The matrix exponential in (
131
) is now defined as the exponential series with the matrix multiplication in (
376
). Using the analogue of (
I.1
) for
K_{a_{1}a_{2}}
, the relation (
135
) becomes

\displaystyle\eta(R_{1})\,\eta(R_{2})\;{}^{\overline{R}_{1}\overline{R}_{2},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}\mskip-1.5mu\exp\,\bigl[M_{b_{1}b_{2}}L\bigr]\,{}^{\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}F_{b_{1}b_{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad=\eta(R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1})\,\eta(R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2})\;{}^{\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}F_{b_{1}b_{2}}\;{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2},\overline{R}_{1}\overline{R}_{2}}\mskip-1.5mu\exp\,\bigl[M_{a_{1}a_{2}}L\bigr]\,,

(395)

where we have abbreviated the logarithm by
L
. The matrix
M_{a_{1}a_{2}}
is defined by the updated version of (
133
),

\displaystyle{}^{R_{1}R_{2},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}K_{a_{1}a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i})

\displaystyle\qquad=\delta_{R_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}\,\delta_{R_{2}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}\,\bigl[{}^{11}\mskip-1.5muK_{a_{1}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{1};\mu_{1})+{}^{11}\mskip-1.5muK_{a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{2};\mu_{2})\bigr]+{}^{R_{1}R_{2},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}M_{a_{1}a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y})\,,

(396)

where
{}^{11}\mskip-1.5muK_{a}
is the Collins-Soper kernel for a single-parton TMD. The corrected form of (
5.1
) will be given below.

The kernel for the rapidity evolution of DPDFs derives from the collinear soft factor and hence is labelled by a single representation. We thus have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\log\zeta}\,{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F(x_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\,{}^{R_{1}}\!J(\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i})\,{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F(x_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta)

(397)

as update of (
139
), where instead of
{}^{R_{1}}\!J
we could also write
{}^{R_{2}}\!J
.
By contrast, the DGLAP kernels for DPDFs carry two colour indices, and the evolution equation for the first parton scale has the structure

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\log\mu_{1}}\,{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{b_{1},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}{}^{R_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}P_{a_{1}b_{1}}\underset{x_{1}}{\otimes}{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R_{2}}F_{b_{1}a_{2}}\,.

(398)

The Collins-Soper equation (
142
) for the evolution kernel should be modified to

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\log\zeta}\,{}^{RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\!P_{ab}(x;\mu,\zeta)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4}\,\delta_{R\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\,\delta_{ab}\,\delta(1-x)\,{}^{R}\gamma_{J}(\mu)\,,

(399)

and corresponding changes are to be made in (
143
).
As a consequence of charge conjugation invariance, one has

\displaystyle{}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$10\phantom{\overline{1}}\hskip-3.01389pt$}\mskip 1.5mu\scalebox{0.7}{$\overline{10}$}}\mskip-1.5muP_{gg}

\displaystyle={}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$\overline{10}$}\,\scalebox{0.7}{$10\phantom{\overline{1}}\hskip-3.01389pt$}}\mskip-1.5muP_{gg}\,,

\displaystyle{}^{AS}\mskip-1.5muP_{gg}

\displaystyle={}^{S\mskip-1.5muA}\mskip-1.5muP_{gg}=0\,,

(400)

see the discussion after equation (
I.1
).

Short-distance matching.

We now specify how the definitions and main results for the short-distance matching of of DTMDs in section
6
are to be adjusted. The necessary adjustments of intermediate steps and of corollaries are easy to deduce. Starting in section
6.2
, we define colour projections

\displaystyle{}^{R}C_{S,a}(\boldsymbol{z})

\displaystyle=P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}\overline{R}\mskip 1.5muR}^{\underline{s}\,\underline{t}}\;C_{S,a}^{\underline{s}\,\underline{t}}(\boldsymbol{z})\big/m(R)

(401)

for the matching kernel of the TMD soft factor for small
\boldsymbol{z}
. The two representation labels of
P^{\underline{s}\,\underline{t}}
in (
401
) must be conjugate to each other, because
P_{\rule{0.0pt}{3.76735pt}AS}^{\underline{s}\,\underline{t}}\;C_{S,g}^{\underline{s}\,\underline{t}}=P_{\rule{0.0pt}{3.76735pt}S\mskip-1.5muA}^{\underline{s}\,\underline{t}}\;C_{S,g}^{\underline{s}\,\underline{t}}=0
due to charge conjugation invariance, and because the double indices
\underline{s}
and
\underline{t}
are either both in the fundamental or both in the adjoint representation. From (
157
) we then obtain

\displaystyle{}^{R_{1}R_{2},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}S_{a_{1}a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y})

\displaystyle=\delta_{R_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}\,\delta_{R_{2}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}{}^{R_{1}}C_{S,a_{1}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{1})\,{}^{R_{2}}C_{S,a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{2})\;{}^{R_{1}}\mskip-1.5muS(\boldsymbol{y})

(402)

instead of (
159
). The matching of the twist-two operators at small
\boldsymbol{z}
reads

\displaystyle{O}^{\underline{r}}_{a}(x,\boldsymbol{y},\boldsymbol{z})

\displaystyle=\sum_{b}C_{\text{us},ab}^{\underline{r}\,\underline{s}}(x^{\prime},\boldsymbol{z})\,\underset{x}{\otimes}{O}^{\underline{s}}_{b}(x^{\prime},\boldsymbol{y})\,.

(403)

In terms of the colour projected matching coefficients

\displaystyle{}^{RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\mskip-1.5muC_{\text{us},ab}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathcal{N}_{a}}{\mathcal{N}_{b}}\,\frac{\varepsilon(R)}{\varepsilon(R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime})}\,\frac{1}{m(R)}\,P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}\overline{R}\,\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}^{\underline{r}\,\underline{s}}\,C_{\text{us},ab}^{\underline{r}\,\underline{s}}\,,

(404)

one obtains the corrected form of (
161
) as

\displaystyle{}^{R}O^{\underline{r}}_{a}(x,\boldsymbol{y},\boldsymbol{z})

\displaystyle=\sum_{b,R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}{}^{R\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\mskip-1.5muC_{\text{us},ab}(x^{\prime},\boldsymbol{z})\,\underset{x}{\otimes}P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}R\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}^{\underline{r}\,\underline{s}}\,{}^{R^{\prime}}\mskip-1.5muO^{\underline{s}}_{b}(x^{\prime},\boldsymbol{y})

(405)

for the colour projected operators defined in (
373
). The possibility of quark-gluon mixing requires two colour labels
R
and
R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}
for
C_{\text{us},ab}
, in contrast to
C_{S,a}
. Adjusting the following steps in the manuscript and defining

\displaystyle{}^{RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\mskip-1.5muC_{ab}(x,\boldsymbol{z};\mu,Y_{C})

\displaystyle=\lim_{Y_{L}\to-\infty}\frac{{}^{RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\mskip-1.5muC_{\text{us},ab}(x,\boldsymbol{z};\mu,Y_{L})}{\sqrt{{}^{R}C_{S,a}(\boldsymbol{z};\mu,2Y_{C}-2Y_{L})\rule{0.0pt}{8.1805pt}}}\,,

(406)

one finds that the correct colour structure for the matching of a DTMD onto a DPDF is

\displaystyle{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y})

\displaystyle=\sum_{b_{1}b_{2},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}{}^{R_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}C_{a_{1}b_{1}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{1})\underset{x_{1}}{\otimes}{}^{R_{2}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}C_{a_{2}b_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{2})\underset{x_{2}}{\otimes}{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}F_{b_{1}b_{2}}(\boldsymbol{y})\,,

(407)

where for brevity, we only give the transverse position arguments. The full version is given in (
I.2
) below. Due to charge conjugation invariance, one has

\displaystyle{}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$10\phantom{\overline{1}}\hskip-3.01389pt$}\mskip 1.5mu\scalebox{0.7}{$\overline{10}$}}C_{gg}

\displaystyle={}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$\overline{10}$}\,\scalebox{0.7}{$10\phantom{\overline{1}}\hskip-3.01389pt$}}C_{gg}\,,

\displaystyle{}^{AS}C_{gg}

\displaystyle={}^{S\mskip-1.5muA}C_{gg}=0\,,

(408)

and analogous relations for
C_{\text{us},gg}
.

The definitions of the kernel
{}^{R}\mskip-1.5muK_{a}
for the rapidity dependence of
{}^{R}C_{S,a}
and of the associated anomalous dimension
{}^{R}\mskip-1.5mu\gamma_{a}
remain as given in (
169
) and (
173
). Equation (
170
) hence becomes

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\log\zeta}\,{}^{RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\mskip-1.5muC_{ab}(x,\boldsymbol{z};\mu,\zeta)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\,{}^{R}\mskip-1.5muK_{a}(\boldsymbol{z};\mu)\,{}^{RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\mskip-1.5muC_{ab}(x,\boldsymbol{z};\mu,\zeta)\,,

(409)

whereas the limiting expression (
171
) for small
\boldsymbol{z}_{i}
should be replaced with

\displaystyle{}^{R_{1}R_{2},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}\mskip-1.5muK_{a_{1}a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i})

\displaystyle\qquad=\delta_{R_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}\,\delta_{R_{2}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}\,\bigl[\mskip 1.5mu{}^{R_{1}}\mskip-1.5muK_{a_{1}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{1};\mu_{1})+{}^{R_{2}}\mskip-1.5muK_{a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{2};\mu_{2})+{}^{R_{1}}\!J(\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i})\bigr]\,.

(410)

Charge conjugation invariance implies
{}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$10\phantom{\overline{1}}\hskip-3.01389pt$}}C_{S,g}={}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$\overline{10}$}}C_{S,g}
and hence
{}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$10\phantom{\overline{1}}\hskip-3.01389pt$}}K_{g}={}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$\overline{10}$}}K_{g}
.
The representation labels in (
6.2.2
) and (
176
) should be adapted as in (
I.1
) and (
409
), respectively. The corrected forms of (
6.2.2
), (
6.2.2
) and (
6.2.2
) will be given below. The functions
g_{F}
and
g_{K}
introduced in section
6.2.3
must have separate colour labels for the two partons, corresponding to the functions on the r.h.s. of their definitions in (
6.2.3
).

The result (
191
) for the expansion of the soft factor at small
\boldsymbol{z}_{1}
,
\boldsymbol{z}_{2}
and
\boldsymbol{y}
correctly reads

\displaystyle{}^{R_{1}R_{2},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}S_{a_{1}a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},Y)

\displaystyle={}^{R_{1}R_{2},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}C_{S,a_{1}a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},Y)\,.

(411)

The kernel
T_{a_{0}\to a_{1}a_{2}}
for parton-level splitting in (
193
) and (
6.3.1
) should have separate labels
R_{1},R_{2}
for
a_{1},a_{2}
, as do the DPDs. The same holds for the twist-four function
G_{a_{1}a_{2}}
in (
194
) and (
6.3.1
).

In section
7.3.1
, one should replace (
7.3.1
) with

\displaystyle 2\pi\delta(p^{+}-p^{\prime+})\,2p^{+}\,{}^{RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\!\mathcal{M}_{ab}(x,\boldsymbol{z})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mathcal{N}_{b}}\,\frac{1}{m(R)}\,P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}\overline{R}}^{\underline{r}\,\underline{s}}\bigl\langle b,p^{\prime},r^{\prime}\big|\mskip 1.5mu{}^{R}O^{\mskip 1.5mu\underline{s}}_{a}(x,\boldsymbol{y},\boldsymbol{z})\mskip 1.5mu\big|b,p,r\bigr\rangle

\displaystyle=\varepsilon(R)\;\frac{\mathcal{N}_{a}}{\mathcal{N}_{b}}\,\frac{1}{m(R)}\,P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}\overline{R}}^{\underline{r}\,\underline{s}}\,\bigl\langle b,p^{\prime},r^{\prime}\big|\mskip 1.5muO^{\mskip 1.5mu\underline{s}}_{a}(x,\boldsymbol{y},\boldsymbol{z})\mskip 1.5mu\big|b,p,r\bigr\rangle\,,\hskip 40.00006pt

\displaystyle{}^{R}\mskip-1.5mu\mathcal{M}_{S,a}(\boldsymbol{z})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m(R)}\,P_{\rule{0.0pt}{4.82224pt}\overline{R}R}^{\underline{r}\,\underline{s}}\,\bigl\langle 0\big|\mskip 1.5muO_{S,a}^{\mskip 1.5mu\underline{r},\underline{s}}(\boldsymbol{y},\boldsymbol{z})\mskip 1.5mu\big|\mskip 1.5mu0\bigr\rangle

(412)

and the first equation in (
7.3.1
) with

\displaystyle{}^{RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime\prime}}\!\mathcal{M}_{ac}(x,\boldsymbol{z})

\displaystyle=\sum_{b,R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}{}^{R\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\mskip-1.5muC_{\text{us},ab}(x^{\prime},\boldsymbol{z})\,\underset{x}{\otimes}{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime\prime}}\!\mathcal{M}_{bc}(x^{\prime})\,,

(413)

whilst the second equation in (
7.3.1
) remains valid as it stands.
The adjustment of the remaining equations in that section is straightforward.

One-loop kernels.

The kernel matrix (
234
) for Collins-Soper evolution of two-gluon DTMDs should be extended to include all colour channels. We refrain from giving the corresponding expressions here.
In the limit
|\boldsymbol{z}_{1}|,|\boldsymbol{z}_{2}|\ll|\boldsymbol{y}|
, the elements of this matrix can be expressed in terms of the kernels
{}^{R}\mskip-1.5muK_{g}
and
{}^{R}\mskip-1.5muJ_{g}
according to (
I.1
). We find that
{}^{10}\mskip-1.5muK_{g}=0
and
{}^{10}\mskip-1.5muJ={}^{D}\!J
with
{}^{D}\!J
given in (
239
).

The colour factors
c_{ab}
in (
309
) and
c_{a_{0}\to a_{1}a_{2}}
in (
319
) now depend on two representation labels
(R_{1}R_{2})
referring to the parton pairs
(ab)
or
(a_{1}a_{2})
. In addition to the results given in the manuscript, we find that both factors are zero for
R_{1}R_{2}=10\,\overline{10}
and
\overline{10}\,10
. This holds for general number of colours
N
. Both factors are also zero for
R_{1}R_{2}=AS
and
S\mskip-1.5muA
due to charge conjugation invariance.

I.2
Definition and rescaling of the rapidity parameter
\zeta

The definition (
125
) of the rapidity parameter in DPDs refers to the plus-momenta
x_{1}p^{+}
and
x_{2}\mskip 1.5mup^{+}
of the two extracted partons, in generalisation of the definition (
61
) for single-parton TMDs. This leads to a problem in convolutions of DPDFs, which was overlooked and led to a number of mistakes in the original manuscript. The problem concerns the evolution equation (
141
) of DPDFs and the matching equation (
6.2.1
) of DTMDs onto DPDFs. The DPDFs on the right-hand-sides of these equations are to be taken at a fixed central rapidity
Y_{C}
. This is explicit in the steps leading to (
6.2.1
), and a corresponding derivation can be given for (
141
). This implies that in integrals over a momentum fraction of a DPD, the variable
\zeta
does not remain constant but must change such that
Y_{C}
remains constant. Such a rescaling in all convolution integrals is possible, but we find it rather awkward. To avoid it, we express the rapidity dependence of DPDFs by the parameter

\displaystyle\zeta_{p}

\displaystyle=2(p^{+})^{2}\,e^{-2Y_{C}}\,,

(414)

which refers to the proton momentum. We use this parameter also for DTMDs, given their close connection with DPDFs. The handling of the rapidity dependence in DTMDs is then different from the one for single-parton TMDs in the modern literature, notably in
Collins:2011zzd
. We note, however, that (
414
) corresponds to the definition of the
\zeta
parameter in the original work of Collins and Soper
Collins:1981uk
.
The parameter
\zeta_{p}
is then to be used instead of
\zeta
from (
125
) as argument for DTMDs and DPDFs throughout the paper. In the following, we point out equations that change in a non-trivial way.

Defining the analogue of (
414
) for the left-moving proton as

\displaystyle\zeta_{\bar{p}}

\displaystyle=2(\bar{p}^{-})^{2}\,e^{2Y_{C}}\,,

(415)

the product of rapidity parameters is fixed to

\displaystyle\zeta_{p}\mskip 1.5mu\zeta_{\bar{p}}

\displaystyle=s^{2}\,,

(416)

where
s=2p^{+}\bar{p}^{-}
is the squared c.m. energy of the proton-proton collision (neglecting proton mass corrections). In terms of the invariant masses produced by the two hard scatters, one has
s=Q_{1}^{2}\mskip 1.5mu\big/(x_{1}\bar{x}_{1})=Q_{2}^{2}\mskip 1.5mu\big/(x_{2}\mskip 1.5mu\bar{x}_{2})
.

The definitions (
414
) and (
415
) refer to the Collins regulator for rapidity divergences. If one works in a different scheme, such as the
\delta
regulator discussed in appendix
B
, the replacement of
\zeta
by
\zeta_{p}=\zeta/(x_{1}x_{2})
as argument of DPDs must be implemented accordingly.

DTMD evolution.

Equations that involve DTMDs but no DPDFs are correct in the original manuscript and just need to be rewritten to reflect the change of argument in the distributions. In terms of the new rapidity parameter, the renormalisation group equation (
124
) for DTMDs reads

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\log\mu_{1}}\,{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}(x_{i},\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta_{p})

\displaystyle=\gamma_{F,a_{1}}(\mu_{1},x_{1}^{2}\mskip 1.5mu\zeta_{p})\,{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}(x_{i},\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta_{p})

(417)

and in analogy for the derivative w.r.t.
\log\mu_{2}
. The rescaled rapidity parameters
x_{1}\zeta/x_{2}
and
x_{2}\mskip 1.5mu\zeta/x_{1}
in (
5.1
) should hence be replaced with
x_{1}^{2}\mskip 1.5mu\zeta_{p}
and
x_{2}^{2}\mskip 1.5mu\zeta_{p}
, respectively.

Some attention is needed when choosing initial conditions for Collins-Soper evolution. In the matching relations for small
\boldsymbol{y}
discussed in section
6.3
, the rapidity parameter dependence of the DTMD arises from a short-distance matching kernel, which does not know about the proton momentum and hence can depend on
x_{1}x_{2}\mskip 1.5mu\zeta_{p}
but not on
\zeta_{p}
. Taking that kernel at a fixed scale
\zeta_{0}
so as to minimise corrections from higher orders thus gives the DTMD at
\zeta_{p}=\zeta_{0}/(x_{1}x_{2})
. Assuming that a corresponding initial condition is also useful at large
\boldsymbol{y}
, we therefore rewrite (
5.1
) in the form

\displaystyle{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}(x_{i},\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta_{p})

\displaystyle\quad=\exp\,\biggl\{\int_{\mu_{01}}^{\mu_{1}}\frac{d\mu}{\mu}\,\biggl[\gamma_{a_{1}}(\mu)-\gamma_{K,a_{1}}(\mu)\,\log\frac{x_{1}\sqrt{\zeta_{p}}}{\mu}\mskip 1.5mu\biggr]+{}^{11}\mskip-1.5muK_{a_{1}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{1};\mu_{01})\log\frac{\sqrt{x_{1}x_{2}\mskip 1.5mu\zeta_{p}}}{\sqrt{\zeta_{0}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\hskip 15.00002pt+\int_{\mu_{02}}^{\mu_{2}}\frac{d\mu}{\mu}\,\biggl[\gamma_{a_{2}}(\mu)-\gamma_{K,a_{2}}(\mu)\,\log\frac{x_{2}\sqrt{\zeta_{p}}}{\mu}\mskip 1.5mu\biggr]+{}^{11}\mskip-1.5muK_{a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{2};\mu_{02})\log\frac{\sqrt{x_{1}x_{2}\mskip 1.5mu\zeta_{p}}}{\sqrt{\zeta_{0}}}\,\biggr\}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}{}^{R_{1}R_{2}\,\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}\mskip-1.5mu\exp\,\biggl[M_{a_{1}a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y})\log\frac{\sqrt{x_{1}x_{2}\mskip 1.5mu\zeta_{p}}}{\sqrt{\zeta_{0}}}\mskip 1.5mu\biggr]

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\times{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}\bigl(x_{i},\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{01},\mu_{02},\zeta_{0}/(x_{1}x_{2})\bigr)\,.

(418)

Correspondingly, the expression (
5.1
) for the cross section level becomes

\displaystyle W_{a_{1}a_{2}b_{1}b_{2}}=\exp\,\biggl\{\int_{\mu_{01}}^{\mu_{1}}\frac{d\mu}{\mu}\,\biggl[\gamma_{a_{1}}(\mu)-\gamma_{K,a_{1}}(\mu)\,\log\frac{Q_{1}^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\biggr]+{}^{11}\mskip-1.5muK_{a_{1}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{1};\mu_{01})\log\frac{Q_{1}Q_{2}}{\zeta_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad\hskip 55.00008pt+\int_{\mu_{02}}^{\mu_{2}}\frac{d\mu}{\mu}\,\biggl[\gamma_{a_{2}}(\mu)-\gamma_{K,a_{2}}(\mu)\,\log\frac{Q_{2}^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\biggr]+{}^{11}\mskip-1.5muK_{a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{2};\mu_{02})\log\frac{Q_{1}Q_{2}}{\zeta_{0}}\,\biggr\}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Phi(\nu\boldsymbol{y}_{+})\,\Phi(\nu\boldsymbol{y}_{-})\sum_{R_{1}R_{2}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}\eta(R_{1})\,\eta(R_{2})\;{}^{\overline{R}_{1}\overline{R}_{2}}F_{b_{1}b_{2}}\bigl(\bar{x}_{i},\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{01},\mu_{02},\zeta_{0}/(\bar{x}_{1}\bar{x}_{2})\bigr)\,

\displaystyle\qquad\times{}^{R_{1}R_{2},\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}\mskip-1.5mu\exp\,\biggl[M_{a_{1}a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y})\log\frac{Q_{1}Q_{2}}{\zeta_{0}}\,\biggr]\,{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}\bigl(x_{i},\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{01},\mu_{02},\zeta_{0}/(x_{1}x_{2})\bigr)\,.

(419)

Analogous variable substitutions should be made in equations (
131
), (
6.2.2
), (
6.2.3
), (
6.3.2
) and (
6.3.2
).
Appropriate modifications should also be made for the rapidity parameters in the last sentence of section
5.3
and throughout section
6.5
, including equations (
6.5
), (
208
), and (
213
).

DPDF evolution.

The corrected DGLAP equation (
141
) for DPDFs is

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\log\mu_{1}}\,{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}(x_{1},x_{2},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta_{p})

\displaystyle\qquad=2\sum_{b_{1},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}{}^{R_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}\mskip-1.5muP_{a_{1}b_{1}}(x_{1}^{\prime};\mu_{1},x_{1}^{2}\mskip 1.5mu\zeta_{p})\underset{x_{1}}{\otimes}{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R_{2}}F_{b_{1}a_{2}}(x_{1}^{\prime},x_{2},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta_{p})\,,

(420)

where the rapidity argument of the evolution kernel
{P}
is rescaled in the same way as the anomalous dimensions in (
417
), thus referring to the plus-momentum of the parton associated with the renormalization scale
\mu_{1}
. An equation analogous to (
I.2
) holds of course for the derivative w.r.t.
\log\mu_{2}
.

With the corrected DGLAP equations for DPDFs, equations (
5.2
) to (
146
) must be modified as well. A correct way to separate the
\zeta
dependence is

\displaystyle{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}(x_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta_{p})

\displaystyle\quad=\exp\,\biggl[\mskip 1.5mu{}^{R_{1}}\!J(\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i})\,\log\frac{\sqrt{\zeta_{p}}}{\sqrt{\zeta_{0}\rule{0.0pt}{7.3194pt}}}\mskip 1.5mu\biggr]\;{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}(x_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta_{0})

\displaystyle\quad=\exp\,\biggl[\mskip 1.5mu{}^{R_{1}}\!J(\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i})\,\log\frac{\sqrt{\zeta_{p}}}{\sqrt{\zeta_{0}\rule{0.0pt}{7.3194pt}}}-\int_{\mu_{0}}^{\mu_{1}}\frac{d\mu}{\mu}\,{}^{R_{1}}\gamma_{J}(\mu)\,\log\frac{\sqrt{\zeta_{0}}}{\mu}-\int_{\mu_{0}}^{\mu_{2}}\frac{d\mu}{\mu}\,{}^{R_{1}}\gamma_{J}(\mu)\,\log\frac{\sqrt{\zeta_{0}}}{\mu}\mskip 1.5mu\biggr]

\displaystyle\qquad\times{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}\widehat{F}_{a_{1}a_{2},\,\mu_{0},\zeta_{0}}(x_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i})\,,\phantom{\frac{1}{1}}

(421)

where
{\widehat{F}}
obeys the evolution equation

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\log\mu_{1}}\,{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}\widehat{F}_{a_{1}a_{2},\,\mu_{0},\zeta_{0}}(x_{1},x_{2},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{1},\mu_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad=-\;{}^{R_{1}}\gamma_{J}(\mu_{1})\log x_{1}\,{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}\widehat{F}_{a_{1}a_{2},\,\mu_{0},\zeta_{0}}(x_{1},x_{2},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{1},\mu_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+2\sum_{b_{1},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}{}^{R_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}\mskip-1.5muP_{a_{1}b_{1}}(x_{1}^{\prime};\mu_{1},\mu_{1}^{2})\underset{x_{1}}{\otimes}{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R_{2}}\widehat{F}_{b_{1}a_{2},\,\mu_{0},\zeta_{0}}(x_{1}^{\prime},x_{2},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{1},\mu_{2})

(422)

and its analogue for
\mu_{2}
, with the initial condition

\displaystyle{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}\widehat{F}_{a_{1}a_{2},\,\mu_{0},\zeta_{0}}(x_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{0},\mu_{0})={}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}(x_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{0},\mu_{0},\zeta_{0})

\displaystyle\quad=\exp\,\Bigl[\mskip 1.5mu{}^{R_{1}}\!J(\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{0},\mu_{0})\,\log\sqrt{x_{1}x_{2}\rule{0.0pt}{6.88889pt}}\mskip 1.5mu\Bigr]\,{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}\bigl(x_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{0},\mu_{0},\zeta_{0}/(x_{1}x_{2})\bigr)\,.

(423)

In (
I.2
) it is essential that
\zeta_{0}
does
not
depend on
x_{1}
or
x_{2}
, so that the initial condition (
I.2
) is taken at constant
\zeta_{p}=\zeta_{0}
. In the second line of that equation, we have indicated that this can of course be related to an initial condition at
\zeta_{p}=\zeta_{0}/(x_{1}x_{2})
by another step of Collins-Soper evolution.

Reflecting these changes, the last sentence in section
7.3.5
should be modified as follows:
“One can thus adapt numerical code for the one-loop evolution of colour singlet DPDs by rescaling the evolution kernels and adding the term with
{}^{R_{1}}\gamma_{J}
from (
I.2
).”

Matching DTMDs to DPDFs.

The corrected master formula (
6.2.1
) for the matching of a DTMD at large
\boldsymbol{y}
reads

\displaystyle{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}(x_{i},\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta_{p})=\sum_{b_{1}b_{2},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}{}^{R_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}C_{a_{1}b_{1}}(x_{1}\!\mskip-1.5mu\smash{{}^{\prime}},\boldsymbol{z}_{1};\mu_{1},x_{1}^{2}\mskip 1.5mu\zeta_{p})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\underset{x_{1}}{\otimes}{}^{R_{2}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}C_{a_{2}b_{2}}(x_{2}\!\mskip-1.5mu\smash{{}^{\prime}},\boldsymbol{z}_{2};\mu_{2},x_{2}^{2}\mskip 1.5mu\zeta_{p})\underset{x_{2}}{\otimes}{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}F_{b_{1}b_{2}}(x_{i}\!\mskip-1.5mu\smash{{}^{\prime}},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta_{p})\,.

(424)

Corresponding substitutions for the rescaled rapidity parameters must be made in (
176
) and (
G
). Moreover, equation (
6.2.2
) is incorrect and should be replaced with

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\log\mu}{}^{RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime\prime}}\mskip-1.5muC_{ac}(x,\boldsymbol{z};\mu,\zeta)=\gamma_{F,a}(\mu,\zeta)\,{}^{RR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime\prime}}\mskip-1.5muC_{ac}(x,\boldsymbol{z};\mu,\zeta)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-2\sum_{b,R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\;\int_{x}^{1}\frac{dx^{\prime}}{x^{\prime}}\;{}^{R\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}}\mskip-1.5muC_{ab}(x^{\prime},\boldsymbol{z};\mu,\zeta)\;{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}\mskip-1.5muR^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime\prime}}\!P_{bc}\biggl(\frac{x}{x^{\prime}};\mu,\frac{\zeta}{x^{\prime\mskip 1.5mu2}}\mskip 1.5mu\biggr)\,,

(425)

which involves a special rescaling of the rapidity parameter in the evolution kernel
{P}
.
The corrected form of (
6.2.2
) is

\displaystyle{}^{R_{1}R_{2}}F_{a_{1}a_{2}}(x_{i},\boldsymbol{z}_{i},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{i},\zeta_{p})

\displaystyle\quad=\exp\,\Biggl\{\int_{\mu_{01}}^{\mu_{1}}\frac{d\mu}{\mu}\,\biggl[\gamma_{a_{1}}(\mu)-\gamma_{K,a_{1}}(\mu)\log\frac{x_{1}\sqrt{\zeta_{p}}}{\mu}\mskip 1.5mu\biggr]+{}^{R_{1}}\mskip-1.5muK_{a_{1}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{1};\mu_{01})\log\frac{x_{1}\sqrt{\zeta_{p}}}{\mu_{01}}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\;\;+\int_{\mu_{02}}^{\mu_{2}}\frac{d\mu}{\mu}\,\biggl[\gamma_{a_{2}}(\mu)-\gamma_{K,a_{2}}(\mu)\log\frac{x_{2}\sqrt{\zeta_{p}}}{\mu}\mskip 1.5mu\biggr]+{}^{R_{2}}\mskip-1.5muK_{a_{2}}(\boldsymbol{z}_{2};\mu_{02})\log\frac{x_{2}\sqrt{\zeta_{p}}}{\mu_{02}}\,\Biggr\}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{b_{1}b_{2},R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}{}^{R_{1}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}}C_{a_{1}b_{1}}(x_{1}^{\prime},\boldsymbol{z}_{1};\mu_{01},\mu_{01}^{2})\underset{x_{1}}{\otimes}{}^{R_{2}\overline{R}{}^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}C_{a_{2}b_{2}}(x_{2}^{\prime},\boldsymbol{z}_{2};\mu_{02},\mu_{02}^{2})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\underset{x_{2}}{\otimes}\exp\,\biggl[{}^{R_{1}}\!J(\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{0i})\log\frac{\sqrt{x_{1}^{\prime}x_{2}^{\prime}\mskip 1.5mu\zeta_{p}}}{\sqrt{\zeta_{0}}}\mskip 1.5mu\biggr]\,{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}F_{b_{1}b_{2}}\bigl(x_{i}^{\prime},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{0i},\zeta_{0}/(x_{1}^{\prime}x_{2}^{\prime})\bigr)\,.

(426)

This is quite similar to (
I.2
), but now the initial condition for the rapidity parameter depends on the momentum fractions
x_{1}^{\prime}
and
x_{2}^{\prime}
of the DPD under the convolution integrals with the matching kernels
{C}
. In (
178
) one should replace
\zeta
with
\zeta_{p}
and
\zeta_{0}
with
\zeta_{0}/(x_{1}^{\prime}x_{2}^{\prime})
in the function arguments and in the explicit logarithm. In the cross-section level result (
6.2.2
), one should then replace the last line by

\displaystyle\underset{x_{2}}{\otimes}\bigl[\Phi(\nu\boldsymbol{y})\bigr]^{2}\,\exp\,\biggl[{}^{R_{1}}\!J(\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{0i})\mskip 1.5mu\log\frac{s\mskip 1.5mu\sqrt{x_{1}^{\prime}x_{2}^{\prime}\mskip 1.5mu\bar{x}_{1}^{\prime}\bar{x}_{2}^{\prime}}}{\zeta_{0}}\,\biggr]\,

\displaystyle\qquad\times{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{3}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{4}}F_{d_{1}d_{2}}\bigl(\bar{x}_{i}^{\prime},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{0i},\zeta_{0}/(\bar{x}_{1}^{\prime}\bar{x}_{2}^{\prime})\bigr)\,{}^{R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{1}R^{\mskip 1.5mu\prime}_{2}}F_{c_{1}c_{2}}\bigl(x_{i}^{\prime},\boldsymbol{y};\mu_{0i},\zeta_{0}/(x_{1}^{\prime}x_{2}^{\prime})\bigr)\,,

(427)

where as in (
I.2
) the constant
\zeta_{0}
is rescaled by the momentum fractions of the DPDs under the convolution integrals with the matching kernels.

Given the above modifications regarding
{\widehat{F}_{a_{1}a_{2},\,\mu_{0},\zeta_{0}}}
, we do not see a corrected form of (
6.2.2
) that would be particularly useful, so this equation and its discussion should be discarded.
